    SELECT TOP(1) [l].[Id], [l].[Content], [l].[CourseId], [l].[CreatedAt], [l].[CreatedBy], [l].[Description], [l].[DriveFileId], [l].[Duration], [l].[IsFree], [l].[IsSequential], [l].[Order], [l].[PdfUrl], [l].[Title], [l].[Type], [l].[UpdatedAt], [l].[VideoUrl]

) AS [t]

ORDER BY [t].[Id]
